Mara had never seen an ugly number look so pleased with itself.","Behind her, Cassian sat on a low equipment case while she fastened a clean strip of cloth around the cut on his forearm. She had got him out. He was breathing, arguing, and capable of making an entirely unnecessary comment about her knot. She kept returning to those facts as though one might vanish if she looked away.","\"I can tie it,\" he said.","\"I know. I am enjoying the privilege.\"","His free hand settled around her wrist. \"Mara.\"","\"You may thank me after we have finished being in danger. I intend to be unbearable.\"","\"I had no reason to expect otherwise.\"","She bent and kissed him, briefly and fiercely. Then she turned back to the console before the relief could become something that would stop her moving.","The tower was partly theirs. That was the trouble with partly. Ivo could keep the old observation channel open, but the final operation still belonged to the underground facility beneath the conservatory. Selene's message had arrived over a narrow maintenance line. It showed a schedule, several active terminals, and a demand that Mara abandon the tower.","The threat was real without being unlimited. Only participants whose devices were paired with those terminals and still in contact with them could be affected. There would be no mysterious wave of amnesia across the streets below. The people at risk were particular people in particular rooms, unable to leave while somebody else decided what would happen to their most recent memories.","Mara found that much more frightening than a fairy tale about the whole world forgetting.","\"Why thirty-eight?\" she asked.","Ivo examined the maintenance schedule. \"The next synchronized cycle. They have prepared the terminal group, but the final command cannot be completed through this tower.\"","\"Can we stop the cycle here?\"","\"Not without knowing what the local units will do when the signal disappears. I can block messages. I cannot honestly promise that blocking them is the same as stopping the procedure safely.\"","He hated saying it. Mara could hear the anger in his careful voice.","The old observation channel clicked. Noor came through with a background of voices and a trolley wheel that needed oil.","\"Elena is safe,\" she said before Mara asked. \"She has no active connection to their terminals. Neither does Ewan. We are locating the remaining participants.\"","Mara closed her eyes for half a second. \"Thank you.\"","\"Thank me by telling me exactly what you have.\"","June photographed the schedule. Tessa read the terminal identifiers aloud. Rowan called the contacts who could locate the buildings without relying on the company's altered directory. Their little circle had once consisted of a thief, a duke, and a silver flower. It now included people who could find a missing ambulance, open a school kitchen, or persuade an anxious porter to answer a telephone after nightfall.","None of those skills would have impressed the manufacturer. Together they began to make a map.","Below the tower windows, Asterfall was still alive. A tram moved along the avenue beside the old opera house. Someone had switched on the theater's emergency lights. The building glowed through the rain, its cracked columns and faded magnificence suddenly more useful than the immaculate offices above the square.","Ada Shaw's voice came over the channel. The theater staff had opened the side doors. Volunteers were turning the rehearsal rooms into a waiting area for patients and families. A choir that had been rehearsing for a festival was moving chairs instead. Their conductor was apparently treating the task as a challenging new production.","\"He says we are uniformly late,\" Ada reported. \"Nobody has had the courage to ask for a tempo.\"","Mara laughed. It cleared her head.","They needed three things at once. People had to reach the active terminals. Ivo and Tessa had to stop the final operation through the local controls. June and the independent observers had to preserve records that would otherwise be overwritten during maintenance. Saving only the files would leave people in danger. Saving only the people might leave the next group defenseless against the same machinery under a new name.","The tasks were complementary. They were also an ambitious use of thirty-six remaining minutes.","Cassian stood. His face had become the face the newspapers knew: alert, composed, impossible to distract. Mara knew what it cost him to wear it now.","\"You are coming with me,\" she said.","\"I had hoped you would notice.\"","\"I am not carrying you out of another room.\"","\"An explicit condition. I accept.\"","Rowan handed them a printed plan. The maintenance route beneath the conservatory connected to the abandoned service passage Mara had found in her mother's notebook. The city had bought up the surrounding buildings, merged several offices, and changed the public entrance twice. The older route had survived because maintenance workers still needed to get heavy equipment downstairs.","For years, the people who presided over the institution had treated that route as beneath their notice. Mara had a generous appreciation of such arrogance.","She took the plan. \"Who is with the families?\"","\"Leda Reed,\" June said. \"And Sister Agnes.\"","The names steadied her. Their quest had begun with a missing man and a game of chess. It would not end with everyone reduced to anonymous digits on a console.","On the screen, a new message appeared.","YOU CANNOT SAVE EVERY COPY.","Mara read it, then looked at the opening beside the old observation line. June had connected a camera to a separate recording unit. Its little indicator remained bright and stubbornly ordinary.","\"She is concerned about the copies,\" Mara said.","\"Good,\" June replied. \"It will give her something to do.\"","They did not answer Selene's message. Ivo opened the service exit. The staircase beyond it smelled of dust and warm metal. Mara started down, Cassian close behind her.","At the first landing, he touched her shoulder. She turned, ready to object to whatever noble sacrifice his expression might announce.","He adjusted the feather-shaped pin at her collar.","\"It was catching on the cloth,\" he said.","She looked at his hands, then up at him. \"You chose this moment?\"","\"I am relying on there being others.\"","For a second she wanted to stay in that narrow place between one crisis and the next, where the man she loved could still worry about a pin. Then the radio clicked, and the voices of the city came through: a nurse asking for a stretcher, a driver confirming an address, someone at the theater arguing about the safest way to move a piano.","Mara took Cassian's hand. They went downstairs together.","Behind them, the clock reached thirty-four."],"questions":[{"type":"细节","stem":"Who is directly exposed to the threatened final operation?","options":["Every citizen whose household receives municipal water.","Participants still connected to terminals with which their devices have been paired.","Only the people watching June's public video feed.","Anyone who has ever visited Mercy House."],"answer":1,"explanation":"风险范围是仍接触已配对终端的参与者，不能把网络威胁扩大成全城居民都会失忆。","evidence":[10],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1123,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0085\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷八｜他们记得的世界</div><h1>Thirty-Eight Minutes</h1><p id=\"chapter-0085-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1522\" data-words=\"1522\">clock</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5700\" data-words=\"5700\">on</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8596\" data-words=\"8596\">tower</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1793\" data-words=\"1793\">console</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7270\" data-words=\"7270\">said</a> thirty-eight <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5252\" data-words=\"5252\">minutes</a>. Its doors were high enough for a painted castle, wide enough for a stage carriage, and almost entirely unsuitable for the two volunteers trying to move a trolley in the opposite direction.","\"Turn it,\" said a familiar voice.","\"Which way?\"","\"The way that does not remove my fingers.\"","Lucien Orr emerged from behind a stack of scenery, carrying a toolbox and the same expression of refined disapproval he had worn at the academy. Mara stared at him.","\"I was told you were an excellent assistant,\" he said to Cassian. \"Do try to improve the sample.\"","Cassian took the near end of the trolley. Together they moved it through the doorway. Behind it stood a young woman in a hospital gown, a blanket around her shoulders and a plastic visitor badge fastened to her sleeve.","\"This corridor leads to the theater?\" she asked.","\"The rehearsal rooms,\" Orr said. \"A doctor is waiting. We have no intention of making you participate in an opera.\"","She gave a small, startled laugh. A volunteer took her onwards.","Orr explained that he had come to help move fragile equipment. The word fragile had expanded somewhat since his arrival. He had already carried a tea urn, a wooden saint, and a frightened child who did not wish to be classified as either.","Mara touched his arm. \"Thank you.\"","\"I shall invoice you when the city is less dramatic.\"","He handed her a laminated plan. Several routes marked as open on the public drawing had been closed. One older passage remained accessible behind the conservatory's loading gallery. He had tested the hinges himself.","\"The outer door is sound,\" he said. \"The inner door is decorative.\"","\"My favorite kind.\"","\"I thought so.\"","The radio clicked. Noor's team had reached the first active rooms. Some participants had already left their terminal chairs. Others had not been told they were free to move. A signed form had been displayed as though it authorized indefinite supervision. Noor had set it aside and asked each person what they wanted.","The distinction took seconds to state and longer to make real. Some people needed an escort. Some were frightened of losing their treatment. One woman insisted on taking a photograph of the room before she left, because the last time she complained someone had told her she had imagined it.","\"Let her,\" Mara said.","\"I am,\" Noor replied.","Mara heard the smile in her voice and kept walking.","The loading gallery opened beneath an exhibition room. Through the floorboards came the thud of footsteps and an angry exchange about an order nobody wanted to sign. The emergency had given certain people considerable authority. It had not given all of them enthusiasm for using it.","Cassian stopped at the corner and listened. Mara crouched beside a crate. On its lid, a cheerful sticker declared the contents a gift from a philanthropic corporation. Beneath the sticker, an older shipping mark identified the same manufacturer whose contracts had nearly closed Mercy House.","She had developed an acute distaste for cheerful stickers.","The inner door stood at the end of the gallery. It was covered with an elaborate painted panel: flowers, birds, a ribbon, and a motto about the liberation of knowledge. Its brass lock was new. The wood beneath it was not.","Mara ran one hand over the edge. The panel had been fitted onto an older frame. Whoever had installed the lock had assumed that strengthening the visible part strengthened the whole door.","\"An expensive illusion,\" she murmured.","\"Can you open it?\"","\"It depends whether you require the illusion to survive.\"","He looked up at the motto. \"I am prepared to readjust my expectations.\"","She took out the little tool roll he had given her after the academy. The familiar weight settled into her palm. For a moment she remembered him standing awkwardly beside a prize table, trying to give her something without making it look like a favor she would have to repay.","\"A very good present,\" she said.","\"I am glad it has found a professional use.\"","\"You may hear a rude noise.\"","\"I have heard several.\"","She worked at the old join, keeping the pressure away from the painted surface. The frame gave with a small, satisfying crack. The panel shifted enough to reveal the unfastened catch behind it. No alarm sounded. The wonderful new lock remained completely intact on the piece of door nobody needed to use.","Cassian looked at it, then at her.","\"I would congratulate you,\" he said, \"but I am afraid you would become impossible.\"","\"Too late.\"","Beyond the door, the service stair descended into a lower hall. Tessa needs the status display.\"","\"Describe it.\"","He did, and she found the unit. It was smaller than she expected: a grey case, a narrow screen, and a semiconductor board visible behind an open maintenance flap. Technology that could hold a life hostage did not necessarily look more impressive than the things Ivo repaired on the hospital's kitchen shelf.","Mara read the display aloud. Tessa asked her to repeat one line. Then the old channel carried a quick exchange of technical terms, a click, and the welcome sound of Ivo swearing at a manufacturer rather than the sea.","\"We can see the local terminal group,\" he said. \"That helps.\"","\"Can you stop it?\"","\"Not from this panel. Keep moving.\"","Footsteps sounded above them. Cassian drew Mara into the shallow space behind the stair. They stood close enough for her to feel his breath against her temple. A guard came down, paused beside the open flap, and looked at the display.","He was young. Mara could see the rain on his boots and the little tremor in his hand. He did not look like a man enjoying power. He looked like a man wondering when obedience had become a different kind of decision.","Cassian stepped out before Mara could stop him.","The guard reached for his radio.","\"You know who I am,\" Cassian said. \"You also know that I no longer hold the office that used to make these conversations easy. So I am asking you a question, not giving you an order.\"","The young man's hand remained on the radio.","\"Have you seen the people in those rooms?\"","His eyes moved towards the lower hall.","\"Then you can help them leave. Up close, the leaves were not exquisite. They were hurried green strokes laid over cracks in the plaster. Someone had tried to make an equipment corridor look like part of the garden, and had been sensible enough to stop before the illusion became expensive.","Mara found the outline of a service panel beneath one painted branch. The hinges were hidden, not absent. She opened it, stepped through, and found herself facing Lady Ardent across a room full of quietly working machines.","Selene wore a pale jacket and no jewelry except the diamond Mara had once wanted to steal. A clear partition divided the control desk from a row of terminal cabinets. On one display, names appeared beside changing status symbols. On another, the maintenance clock continued its descent.","\"You came through your mother's door,\" Selene said.","Mara had expected to be afraid of her. Instead she felt a cold, oddly practical anger.","\"You should have put the expensive lock on that one.\"","Selene's gaze moved to Cassian's bandaged arm. \"Your persistence has become costly.\"","\"I have been keeping the receipts,\" he said.","The remark made her smile. Mara disliked the smile. It treated his resistance as a character trait she might find attractive under more convenient circumstances.","Behind Mara, the old radio clicked once. Ivo was listening. She let her hand fall against the side of her coat, where the open microphone remained concealed. A small deception, ordinary enough to escape an adversary interested in grander things.","\"Tell me how to stop the operation,\" Mara said.","\"You do not understand what you are asking to destroy.\"","\"Then use the time you have to explain it.\"","Selene touched a key. The nearest screen changed. Elena appeared in an old recording, seated at a workbench with her hair pinned carelessly above her neck. She was younger than the mother Mara had just recovered, and less tired. She held a piece of circuitry between finger and thumb.","Mara's heart gave one hard, painful beat.","\"This is not a mind,\" Elena said in the recording. \"It is a trace collected under particular conditions. Do not confuse the existence of a record with ownership of the person who produced it.\"","The image stopped.","For a moment Mara could see only her mother's hands. She remembered those hands removing a splinter, stirring sugar into tea, covering a drawing so that a child would not see the unfinished surprise.","Selene watched her. \"She was an idealist. You know what the city did to people who relied on its compassion.\"","\"I know what you did to her.\"","\"You know part of it. You could know more.\"","The next file showed rows of dated entries. Not a restored life. Not a complete childhood. Fragments, observations, pieces selected because someone had thought them useful. Some might help Elena reconstruct an experience. Some might distort it if treated as more than they were.","Mara wanted them anyway. Desire did not become smaller because she understood its danger.","Cassian stood beside her without speaking. She was grateful that he did not tell her the choice was easy.","\"There are records of the accident,\" Selene continued, turning towards him. \"You have spent five years living with questions I could help answer.\"","\"Then preserve the records,\" he said. \"And let independent people examine them.\"","\"People who will reduce a decade of work to a scandal?\"","\"People you cannot dismiss when you dislike the result.\"","Selene's expression hardened. She looked back at Mara.","\"You could have a place in the institution that comes after this. You have the ability. The city already follows you. Imagine what you could accomplish if you stopped behaving as though influence were something to apologize for.\"","It was a better offer than money. Mara knew that immediately. Money could rebuild Mercy House. Influence could keep it from being broken again. Authority could make the next cruel official answer a telephone, the next arrogant donor listen, the next frightened mother find an open door.","And somebody would have to decide which doors stayed closed.","\"You are offering me your chair,\" she said.","\"I am offering you a future.\"","Mara looked past her at the terminal names. Beside one, a symbol changed from occupied to clear. Noor had reached someone. She forced herself not to look again.","\"A future in which people must come to me to discover whether they are allowed to keep their own past?\"","\"You prefer disorder?\"","Mara laughed once. It surprised Selene.","\"You keep offering me two doors and pretending you built the whole world between them.\"","The microphone under her coat remained warm against her palm. June would be recording. Ivo would be checking the displays. The people Selene dismissed as disorder were already doing things she had not permitted.","\"I want the terminal group released,\" Mara said. \"I want the records preserved. And I want you away from the controls.\"","Selene shook her head. \"You have no means of enforcing that.\"","Mara took the silver flower from her pocket. She did not point it at the desk like a weapon. She set it on the edge of the partition, where one petal turned towards a cable running beneath the floor.","Selene's eyes moved to it.","\"That old device cannot give you access.\"","\"No,\" Mara said. \"It tells me where the current is. I have friends who know what to do with that information.\"","Behind the partition, a maintenance light changed color.","Selene turned. Cassian moved at the same moment, stepping between her and the side exit. Mara crossed to the local junction panel. She had not outrun Selene's technology. She had used the minutes Selene spent admiring her own argument.","\"The lower display,\" Tessa said over the radio. \"Read it. Do not touch the switch yet.\"","Mara read the sequence aloud. On the other end of the channel, Ivo repeated the final number.","Selene pressed a key on the desk. The partition door locked with a heavy click.","Cassian looked at the sealed exit, then at Mara. \"I had been hoping to finish without another locked room.\"","\"You should stop choosing such impressive company.\"","He smiled. The expression did not reach his eyes. Behind Selene, another screen had lit up. A terminal operator was asking why the local control had changed.","The clock reached twenty-four minutes.","At Mercy House, Noor's voice came through the radio, quiet and steady.","\"We have the first group out. Keep her talking.\"","Mara looked at Selene's diamond, the perfect jacket, the beautiful room in which other people's lives had become a manageable set of symbols.","\"You wanted to know what I would do with influence,\" she said. \"Watch carefully.\""],"questions":[{"type":"词句理解","stem":"Elena's warning that a trace is “not a mind” stresses the difference between","options":["an old recording and
